Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the nonequilibrium object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 570. The moduli space has dimension at most 610 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_570(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 570. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^610(S, F) is trivial. QED.
